game/index.html|880|html:text|The bestiary plates are the work of six artists and are used here as
        adaptations: each drawing was converted to greyscale and reduced to line
        for the plate frame, and nothing else about it was altered.
        The two module covers and the plates of the bone-dog and the leech are
        adapted the same way from work that is out of copyright by age: Harry
        Clarke's illustrations to Poe's Tales of Mystery and Imagination (1919)
        and to Faust (1925), and Thomas Bewick's wood engravings for A General
        History of Quadrupeds (1790). Clarke died in 1931 and Bewick in 1828,
        and no licence is claimed over either.
        Colorful Monsters &copy; 2020 by Mikoarc Studio is licensed under CC BY 4.0.
        Plates by Justin Nichol are licensed under CC BY 4.0. Plates by Jeff
        Preston are licensed under CC BY 3.0. Plates by Mariana Ruiz Villarreal
        (LadyofHats), Morgan Strauss and Remi are released under CC0.

When it fires against the walking dead, the kill is worth half again the experience, and the words stand a while after the thing is down: every stroke lands 15% heavier and the shrug turns another point and a half aside, for a minute. Under the red moon every beast of the floor walks risen, and the rites answer over all of them.

game/lowmoor.js|1|property:warren ratling|Rat-kin of the warren, bred in the middens under the goblin runs and armed with what the goblins throw away. It stands upright to the height of a child, and it fights in numbers for a share of the carrion it guards.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:goblin archer|Goblins of keener eye are given shortbows and set to watch the approaches. Their arrows fly crooked, but they fly in great number.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:mimic|A patient predator that takes the shape of a chest, a door, a barrel: whatever a tired traveller reaches for. The grip of its hide is the grip of hot pitch, and it eats what it holds.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:clockwork bailiff|The watch of the counterfeit town, built of brass and mainspring behind a stamped mask, about a warrant no living clerk has read. It reads the charge aloud to its prisoners, and the sentence is carried out where they stand.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:griffon|Eagle before and lion behind, proud and carnivorous. The menagerie's stock has hunted its own galleries for a thousand years and knows every perch in the dark.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ink elemental|The scriptorium's ink, pooled and self-willed after an age in the warm channels. It takes the shape of whatever was last written in it, and it writes upon flesh as readily as vellum.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:spectre|The unquiet remainder of one who died in the halls and was catalogued rather than buried. Its touch draws the warmth from the living, and doors are nothing to it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:quicksilver hound|A hunting shape of living quicksilver that runs the gallery floors between the mirrors. A blade passes through it and leaves a ripple, and it gathers itself again with the teeth foremost.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:medusa|A woman crowned with serpents, whose gaze is death in stone. The figures in her gallery stand in attitudes of flight, and the newest of them are travellers the survey has on its rolls.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:waxling|A servant of poured wax, child-sized, winged after the fashion of a bat and warm to the touch. It runs the master's errands still, and some of the errands are for blood.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:flesh golem|A man assembled from men, sewn and quickened upon the foundry's tables. It obeys the last order it was given, and the last order was to keep the tables clear.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:half-made servitor|A casting quickened before its finishing, one arm short and its face left smooth. It works at its own assembly between watches, with whatever parts come to hand.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:stone golem|The foundry's masterwork in dressed stone, patient as its quarry. It stands among the statuary until the floor trembles, and then the statuary walks.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:loosed armature|An arm of the observatory's great model, drawn off its pivot and gone walking down the gallery. It keeps the sweep it was cut for, turning upon nothing at the height of a man's chest, and it cuts what stands in the arc.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:unsleeping warden|The last and heaviest of the foundry's issue, set to keep the observatory door. It has stood its post since the pouring, and its orders admit no callers.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:vacant usher|The office of the court's usher, kept up long after the usher who held it. It stands at the servants' door in a grey livery, hooded and gaunt, and it admits and turns away by a precedence only it now remembers. It strikes those who pass out of their proper order.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shadow|A patch of living dark that peels itself from a wall the lamps of the empty court have left unlit. It drains the strength from those it touches, and what it drains it keeps. The court's antechambers are thick with them, and they cross no lit ground.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:court-crier|The herald of a court that no longer sits, crying the seasons to a room of empty thrones. It gives the order of business in a cracked voice to any who enter, and it takes the interruption of its cry for contempt. Its handbell carries further in the dark than a shout.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ghost|The remainder of one who died attending the court and was never entered as departed. It keeps to the antechambers by the road it walked in life, passing through the shut doors of the offices, and its touch ages the living toward their own entry upon the Roll.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:roll-binder|A thing of thread and lead and long habit that binds the volumes of the Roll as they are filled. It works the stacks by the court's endless day, and it binds into its volumes whatever it finds loose among the shelves: a page, a pen, a reader's hand.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:struck clerk|A clerk of the Roll whose own name was entered and then struck through, and who keeps at the enrolling still. It strikes through the living as it was struck, drawing its pale ink across them, and where the ink falls the strength goes out of the limb.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:roll-thrall|A hand of the Roll risen from the enrolling stool, still in the grey livery of the court. It carries the volume it was entered in, and it comes at a caller with the whole weight of the names that volume holds.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:night hag|A keeper of old bargains that has found in the Roll a manifest of debts to her taste. She reckons the terms of the sealed against the living, and she comes in the dark of a rest to press the count. Her tooth she draws for a reckoning, and the reckoning is always against you.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:tomb-mason|The mason set to cut the eighth tomb of the gallery, kept at the work long after the hand that set it. It dresses and squares the stone by the chalked line and the plumb, and it measures every caller against the figure it has yet to fill. Its maul falls where the sword rings off.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:barrow-servant|A warden's servant, bound in tomb-linen and laid up in the gallery to keep the tombs it served in life. It rises at a disturbance of the grave-goods and comes on without haste, and the rot of its wrapping carries a curse that stiffens the sinew of the living.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:stone mourner|A funeral effigy of the gallery, carved crouching with its hands cupped for an offering, that walks when the offering is withheld. It keeps to the tombs it mourns and returns to its post between, and the weight of its stone hands falls as a mason's block falls.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:mummy lord|A warden of the eldest strata, laid in the gallery under mask and seal, that keeps the count of its tomb though its seal was drawn an age ago. It rises whole, commands the lesser dead of the gallery, and its grave-mask hides a face that death has not been let to finish.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:chain devil|A thing of animate chain at home among the great chains of the debtors' deep, where it can scarce be told from the links until the links take hold. It binds and draws its prey to the wall as a debtor is bound to a term, and its barbs are cut to hold and not to loose.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:debt-thrall|A debtor of the deep risen from the cell it filed its way out of, still dragging the term it could not serve. It comes on bound in a sprung shackle, and it fights to bind another in its place, that the count of the cells may stand even. What is left of the debtor within is carried along.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:tally-hound|A hyena-headed runner of the counting-house, set down the debtors' gallery to keep the tally of who comes and goes. It counts the living against the manifest by scent, gives tongue at a name it cannot find, and courses in silence at a name it can. Its collar is notched for every quarry it has entered.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:bone devil|A gaoler-fiend of the deep prison, hook-limbed and patient, set to keep the debtors to their cells. It works the gallery on a warder's round, and its sting carries a venom that turns the wound cold and the count against you. It herds before it kills.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:works-tender|The last hand upon the sealing-works, greasing the chains and reading the gauges to the drum's own count. It suffers nothing near the beam and the pumps, and it carries what it fells off to the melt to be poured again, and its rag is black to the elbow.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:iron golem|A warden of dressed iron built to keep the sealing-works, patient as the beam it stands beside. It moves only when the works are threatened, and then it moves with the whole mass of its make. The floor tells its coming a room ahead through the boots.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:water elemental|The gathered weeping of the seal, given a shape and a will by an age of being driven back against the wound. It rises from the channels of the works, breaks over a caller in a cold tower, and drags what it takes down the gutters toward the grating. It keeps to the wet.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:steam mephit|A small foul thing of the sealing-works, condensed out of the steam the drum drives back against the wound. It rides the gauge-lines and bursts when it is broken, and the burst carries the heat of the works with it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:salamander|A serpent of living fire kept in the Last Vault against the hour the jar is fed to the flame. It coils about the laid hearth, warms the black stone of the vault, and takes any that near the mantel for the tender come at last to light the fire. Its coil sears through mail.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:last clerk|The Lich's own clerk, the last but one upon the Roll, kept at the enrolling of a new season that never comes. It sits the vault's second desk, copies the count in a fair hand, and defends the record before its own unlife. Its inkhorn is dried to a crust in a grip that will not open.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:seal-keeper|The warden of the seven seals hung upon the vault wall, that strikes each through in lead as its warden falls and keeps the seventh whole. It fights to keep the seventh seal from the striking, for the striking of the seventh is the count left off. Its signet-die cuts the impression of a door that opens once.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:xorn|A three-armed eater of the deep stone and its treasures, come up through the black walls of the Last Vault by the smell of the hoard below. It takes coin and gem and the metal of a blade alike, rising through the floor to feed and sinking back when it is full. Stone is no wall to it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sill-shade|A shade of the dark that pours over the sill of the last door with the reversed water. It keeps to the threshold no warden crossed living, and it draws the warmth from those who cross where it has pooled. It belongs to the door, and a caller who wins past the sill is quit of it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:brink-lurker|A low grey crawler that keeps the very brink of the broken seal, where the reversed water goes over and down. It has fed at that edge since the water first went over, and it fastens on a boot at the sill and hauls, to send its meat after the water into the drop.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:spillwrack|A mass of drowned matter gathered together and given a slow will by the water that pours forever over the sill. It drifts down the wet stone in the shape of a man face-down, and it winds about the legs of a wader and bears the weight downward.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sluiceling|A thing born of the water driven back on every floor above and let go at last over the sill. It runs in the sheet of the fall, shaped like nothing the eye can fix upon, and it strikes with the whole weight of the water behind it before it goes on down.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:earth elemental|A power of the living rock, woken in the drowned foundation where the oldest stone was worked from below. It rises out of a wall or a floor without warning and returns to it as easily, and its blows carry the mass of the mountain the Maw was dug into.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:foundation-slough|A slough of the drowned foundation, shed from the oldest course of stone and gone about on its own account. It fills a gallery from wall to wall in the black water, and what it closes upon it takes down to the bone by degrees.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:foundation-wright|A mason-thing of the far side, drowned at the raising of the foundation and raising it yet, that cuts the deepest stone upward toward a surface it will never reach. It defends the course it is cutting against any that near it, and its inverted chisel swings with the reach of a thing built to cut above its own head.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:drowned mason|A journeyman of the foundation, drowned at the work and working still, its mallet swinging slow beneath the black water. It keeps to the course it was cutting and defends it, and its mallet falls slow and heavy, timed to a work that has no end.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:blankling|A smooth-faced thing of the unwritten floor, featureless as the stone that takes no mark. It keeps no shape the eye can hold and leaves no print, and it presses itself upon a caller to take the marks off them, the face and the name and the record, and to leave them as blank as the floor.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:blank scrivener|A clerk of the unwritten floor gone as blank as the stone it keeps, that unwrites where a clerk would write. It sets a cold hand upon a caller and lifts the record off them, the ink of a name rising from the skin, and the arm it has read goes slack and slow.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:erasure|A creeping haze of the unwritten floor that takes the record off whatever it crosses. It moves without haste over the blank stone, and it unwrites a caller the way a wet cloth unwrites a slate, and what it has crossed the floor keeps no memory of.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:fetterling|A small iron thing of the chained dark, cast off the great chain where a link was drawn open. It scuttles the slack miles on legs of parted links, and it fastens on a caller as a fetter fastens, and it is far stronger than its size, for it is a piece of a thing that broke the world's own chain.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:anchorite|A thing walled into the chained dark at an anchor of the great chain, in penance an age gone, and grown into the iron it was chained to. It bides at its anchor where the chain lies slack, and it reaches the length of its parted bond to strike a caller who passes within, cold as the iron and stronger.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:behir|A great serpent of the deep dark, thick as a man's waist and crackling with a caged lightning, that has denned in the slack miles of the chain since the fetter parted. It runs the links faster than a horse and swallows its prey whole, and the storm of its breath fills a gallery from wall to wall.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:iron penitent|A thing chained in the dark an age ago in penance for a debt, and loosed at last when the great chain parted, dragging its own parted iron behind it. It comes on slow and heavy and full of an old grievance, and it fights to fasten its loosed chain upon another and stand free.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:tally-shade|A shade of the counting, thin as a scratched line, that keeps to the tally-marks cut into the waking ground. It rises from the cuts in sevens and draws itself across a caller like a mark tallied, and each stroke of it takes a measure of the strength, as a tally takes a measure of a debt.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:stir-scale|A warm-plated crawler of the waking ground that lies buried in the bedrock and rouses only when the ground stirs beneath a foot. It comes up where the stone breathes and sinks when the breath falls even, and it strikes with the heat of the deep still in its plates, hot off the glow below.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ground-drake|A great burrower of the waking ground, plated along the back with scales that hold the heat of the deep stone. It swims the bedrock as a fish swims water, drawn upward by the warmth of a living body, and what it closes upon is seared in the closing.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sum-taker|A horned thing of the waking ground, kept at the tally the ground has kept since before Mereward. It takes a caller for an error in the count, to be struck out and set right, and it comes on with the whole authority of a sum that must balance.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:coin-shade|A gold-lit shade risen from the warm coin of the Bottom, thin as the glow of the thing breathing beneath. It keeps to the hoard it was struck for, and it pulls the heat out of a caller into the cold gold it wears, and sinks back into the coin when it has fed.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:purple worm|A vast blind worm of the deepest deep, the girth of a cart and longer than the eye can follow, that swims the coin of the Bottom as through loose earth. It takes a caller whole with a mouth that fills a gallery, and its sting is a spar of chitin heavy with a venom the sun has never weakened.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:last debtor|The greatest of the debts the deep was dug to bury, risen from the coin at the Bottom to stand between you and the thing beneath it. It is a body grown vast upon the warm hoard, counting, and it fights to keep the last count uncalled, for the calling of it is the collecting.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:hoard-golem|A giant of the first hoard, coin fused into coin in the heat of the thing breathing below, walking on legs of run gold. It strikes with the whole warm mass of the hoard, the coin of it rings at every blow, and it gathers back into the hoard whatever it fells.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sump crawler|A blind, flat-bodied thing of the flooded lower workings, the length of a man's arm and armoured in a slick grey shell. It lies buried in the silt of the sumps until the damp rises through the mine, and then it comes up the seeping galleries to hunt in the wet, sinking back into the silt when the water falls again.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shale-lizard|A great plated lizard of the deep roads, its back a shield of grey shale grown over long ages. It lies dormant in the rubble of the lower halls, and the tremor of the deep stone rouses it: when the roots of the world shift, the shale-lizard comes grinding up out of the scree to feed.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:rimewing|A pale broad-winged thing of the cold manor halls, ragged at the pinion and dusted with frost. It keeps to the lenses and the mirrors, still as a smear of grey, and takes the air only while the breathing dark moves cold through the halls. Where it has passed, the gilt is left rimed and the glass gone white.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gaol-crawler|A blind flat-bodied thing of the flooded lower cells, armoured in a rasped grey plate and the length of a tall man. It lies buried in the silt of the debtors' deep until the beam of the sealing-works sets the deep stone shaking, and then it comes grinding up the drowned galleries to hunt while the tremor holds, sinking back into the silt when the beat falls even again.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:coin-wyrm|A long gold-scaled serpent that swims the warm coin of the Bottom as a snake swims sand, seen only when the deep air lies dead and still and close. It surfaces in the calm to bask in the glow of the thing breathing, its scales struck from the same coin it swims, and it sinks back into the hoard the moment the air stirs. Where it has passed, the coin lies warmer, and the glow shows the track of it a while.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:moor-hound|A lean hound of the open moor, peat-black and long in the leg, that hunts the road in threes and fours. It runs its quarry as far as the standing mist and turns back there, for it will not cross the line the birds will not cross.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:bog-lantern|A cold thing of the peat that carries its own pale light at a lantern's height and moves at a walking pace. It leads toward the deep cuttings and holds its place at the lip of them until whoever follows is committed to the ground.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shutter-thrall|A villager of Hollowmere kept past their dying by the house upon the crag, and sent back down the hill to bar the shutters from the street side. It works by night at the work it did in life, and it answers to its own name.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:wererat|A man of the Vale who takes the shape of a rat at need and keeps the cunning of the man in either shape. It runs the drains and the thatch of Hollowmere, and it carries word up the crag for the price of being let alone.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:grave-verger|The dead verger of the village chapel, risen with the bell-rope still in hand. It rings for burials no one has ordered, it counts the mourners at the door, and it holds that door against any who would leave before the office is done.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:werebear|A herdsman of the Vale in the shape of a great bear, red-eyed and thick in the shoulder, that keeps to the crag pastures above the village. It comes out of the scrub at a run and bears a man down under its weight.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:tallow-saint|A votive figure of the chandler's making, wax laid over a wooden armature and set up in the shuttered houses against the night. Enough prayer has gone into these to move them, and they move after dark, and they keep the doors shut from the inside.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:kennel-brute|A brute of the castle kennels, kept in the runs on a short chain and fed what the hunt left. It came down into the village lanes when the house was buried, and it keeps the lanes yet, collared still.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:twig blight|A creeping shrub of the drowned wood, quickened by whatever soured the water, that walks upon a knot of root and strikes with barbed shoots. It stands among the true thorn without stirring until a boot comes within its reach.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:needle blight|A taller blight of the Thornwake, pine-needled and the height of a man, that looses its needles in a volley at ten paces. A stand of them fires together, and the volley comes from both sides of the ride at once.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:thorn-gibbet|A gallows of grown thorn, raised where the wood took a hanged man and kept him. It has the reach of the branch and the strength of the trunk, and it lifts whatever it takes clear of the ground before it closes.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:skin-changer|A hunter of the Vale who wears the pelt of a wolf and is no wolf, for the pelt carries no seam and does not come off. It keeps to the drowned rides, it hunts to the horn, and it answers the horn of the castle whenever that horn is blown.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:green hag|An old woman of the standing water, green as pond-weed and strong as a smith, who keeps a hut upon the drowned ride. She trades in favours and in names, and she is bound by the letter of a bargain and by nothing else in the world.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:minotaur skeleton|The bones of the bull kept in the undercroft, harnessed still and moving still, that patrol the orchard rows at a walk. It strikes with the horns and with the weight, and the harness upon it was buckled by a hand that is no longer in the Vale.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:orchard-hand|The remains of one of the abbey's lay brothers, kept at the pruning of the grave-trees. It works the rows in their numbered order and takes up its hook against whoever stands between it and the next tree.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gnoll|A hyena-headed scavenger of the burying-ground, come down out of the hills to the easiest meat in the Vale. It hunts in threes and fours, it digs where the ground is soft, and it fights its own kind over what it digs.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sprite|A small winged thing of the grave-orchard, no longer than a hand and armed with a thorn. It shoots from the branches at the eye and the throat, and the lime of the orchard lies upon its wings and burns where it settles.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:unshriven monk|A brother of Saint Ordel who died with his confession unheard and rose again the same night. It comes upon the living with its hands open, asking the office it was refused, and it treats every refusal as the first.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:banshee|The keening remnant of a lady of the Vale, bound to the orchard where she was buried outside consecrated ground. Her cry carries the length of the valley, and those who hear it close at hand are found in the morning cold and unmarked.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:jackalwere|A creature of the fen that walks as a man by daylight and runs as a jackal by dark, and lies in both shapes. It offers to guide a party across the reeds, and it is a good guide, and it brings them to the deep water by the shortest road.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sea hag|A hag of the black water of Ysmere, though no sea has ever reached this valley. She rises beside a wading party to be looked at, for the sight of her stops the heart of the weak, and she takes the rest under at her own pace.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:drowned bridesmaid|One of the wedding party that walked into Ysmere behind its bride, risen in the linen it drowned in. It keeps to the shallows and reaches for the hand of whoever comes down to the water's edge.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:reed-wraith|A cold shape among the black reeds with the outline of a woman and the substance of a draught. Its touch takes the warmth out of a limb and leaves it dead to the shoulder, and the reeds close behind it unbent.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shambling mound|A moving heap of fen-thatch and rotted reed, taller than a man and heavier than three. It smothers rather than strikes, and both rain and lightning feed it, and the Vale provides a great deal of the first.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:nightmare|A black horse of the fen with a burning mane and hooves that scorch the turf, kept for the castle's use and pastured among the reeds. It is caught by no one, it goes where it pleases, and it comes to the horn of the crag.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:quasit|A small fiend of the curtain wall, horned and barbed and quick as a bat in the dark. It goes out of sight between one step and the next, and it comes back inside the guard of the man it left.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:hippogriff|An eagle before and a horse behind, nesting upon the curtain wall of the buried house. It stoops upon whatever crosses the open ground below the wall, and it carries what it takes back to the nest to be dealt with there.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:wall-warden|A guardsman of the curtain wall, frozen at his post the night the house was buried and standing there yet. He challenges by the old form, he treats silence as a wrong answer, and he has treated every answer as a wrong answer since.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:minotaur|A bull-headed giant kept in the undercroft below the gatehouse, whose passages were cut to hold it. It has learned every turn of those cellars, it hunts them by scent, and it keeps at the hunt for as long as the hunt lasts.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:kennel-thrall|One of the Count's under-kennelmen, kept at his work past his dying, who feeds the runs to the hour and shuts the gates behind him. He fights with the whip and the boar-spear, and the pack comes in when he is hard pressed.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:nothic|A hunched thing of the castle cellars, chained there by a hand that wanted it kept rather than killed. It was a scholar of the house once. It reads whatever it looks at and says the reading aloud, and its gaze rots the flesh it reads.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:portrait-thing|What comes out of the long gallery's canvases when the light is low, wearing the face the painter gave it. It moves at the pace of a walking man and takes the same road each time, and the canvas it left stands empty until it returns.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:night-bride|One of the women of Hollowmere taken up the crag and kept, risen pale and quick and bound to the house. She is met upon the great stair in marriage-lace, and she asks whether you have come for the wedding.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:incubus|A fiend of the castle's upper rooms that visits the sleeping and takes what it needs a little at a time. It wears whatever face the sleeper trusts, and it has had three hundred years to learn which faces those are.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:rakshasa|A fiend in the shape of a man with a tiger's head and hands turned backward at the wrist, seated in the Count's own library as a guest of long standing. It was party to the pact that closed the Vale, and it has kept its side of that pact exactly.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:vampire|One of the Count's elder get, given a wing of the castle and a coffin of its own and left to keep the house between his risings. It has the strength of ten and the patience of the grave, and it fights to hold the stair until its master wakes.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:giant rat|Strike for the haunches and keep your thumbs from the teeth. The tail is bought whole; a tail in pieces is bought as pieces.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:kobold|It throws four stones and then it runs. Close upon the fifth count and take the tooth unmarked.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:stirge|Carry the torch at arm's length from the body, for the stirge strikes at warmth. Take it upon the backswing, as it lifts.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:cave goblin|It hates the torch and watches the torch, so let the torch do the feinting. Members schooled in Witchlight report the beast follows the light wherever the light is sent.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:goblin cutter|The first rush is the whole of its plan. Give ground one pace, let the rush spend itself, and finish the matter upon the turn.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:warren ratling|They fight for the midden and break off when the midden is fired, so put a torch to the heap and take them as they scatter. Sound pieces of gnawed bone are bought by the piece, so take the whole ones.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:goblin archer|Cross open ground upon a slant, and the crooked arrows cross behind you. The heads of snapped arrows are bought by the dozen at the counter.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:worg|It circles to the blind side by habit, and offers the throat upon the second circle. The Steady Hand, held ready, makes that offer final.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:hobgoblin|It fights by the drill-book, and the book keeps no page for a broken rank. Fell the right-hand man of the file and the file spends its discipline reforming.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:giant spider|Carry the pole before you at head height and the snares announce themselves upon it. Choose your footing before you burn a single strand.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:goblin boss|It fights before an audience, and it treats without one. Bar the gallery door first, and the treating begins at once.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:skeleton|Spare the edge and use the flat, or a mace, at the hip. The Dead Tongue, spoken as the bones rise, unbinds the older sort a stroke early.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:zombie|It follows the last sound it heard, and a thrown stone is a sound. Spend the quiet you buy upon the neck, which is the whole of the work.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ghoul|Keep the bracers high; the paralysis rides in the claw and enters at the bare forearm. Members who hold Sing Them Down report the stiffness passes the sooner.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:animated armour|Its orders are its whole mind, and the orders name a post. Draw it three paces off the post and its footwork stays behind; gather every rivet, for the rivets are the proof.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:spider matron|Burn the egg-sacs first or fight her twice. The silk takes a fair price cut dry and rolled; folded silk is bought as rag.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ghast|The stench arrives two rooms ahead of it; trust the nose over the ear and stand where the air moves. Keep the Old Things Off, held ready, makes the reek bearable and the paralysis brief.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gargoyle|Sound the perches with the pole as you pass: stone rings, and a gargoyle thuds. Smell the Working tells the live stone from the dead at ten paces.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ogre|The club telegraphs from the shoulder, so watch the shoulder and spend the swing. The knuckle is bought whole, by weight, and the weighing draws a crowd.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:wraith|Plain steel passes through it; silvered or rune-etched work bites. The Dead Tongue, spoken plainly, holds it at arm's length for the length of the speaking.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:basilisk|Watch the feet in the polish of your shield and never the head. The eye keeps its virtue a week in honey, and the counter sells honey for this reason.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:manticore|Count the spikes as they fly: the tail carries four and twenty, and a spent manticore must land to fight. Gather the spikes, for the spikes are the trophy and the proof.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ettin|The heads quarrel over precedence at the first wound, so wound it early and keep the middle distance while they settle it. The settling is the opening.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:young white dragon|The cold runs ahead of the breath: when the passage frosts, step wide and count three. Pry the scales cold, for a scale pried warm curls.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:young green dragon|It will offer terms, and the terms are the trap. Decline in silence, and hold your breath through the first green cloud, which is the answer to the declining.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:chimera|Three heads and one belly, and all three answer when the belly is threatened. Feint low: the goat takes the feint first, and the goat is the honest head.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:wyvern|Listen for the drip of venom, which arrives ahead of the wings. Cut the gland standing well behind the sting, and never squeeze it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:revenant|Learn the name of its quarry and stand out of the road between them, for it spends no stroke on bystanders. The grudge is filed at the counter as found; the Guild keeps a drawer.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:vampire spawn|In the open dark it is swift past all parrying, so make it come to the lamp, and the lamp evens the account. Tell-Tale Glow marks the fang worth the taking.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:young black dragon|The acid falls and pools, and the pools keep their virtue for an hour: mark them, and fight between them. Boots are on the Guild's account for members upon this floor.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:darkmantle|Sound the ceiling with the pole before the torch goes under it. A dropped darkmantle smothers the light first, so carry the second candle lit and behind you.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:rust monster|Ground your weapons ten paces back and go in with the torch and a stone club. Members who feed it a spare buckle report it follows the buckle, and the feeler is taken while it dines.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:goblin shard-bearer|It will die for the burden, so take the burden question off the table: pin the pack-strap to the wall and the bearer fights like any goblin. The sliver goes to the counter in the tongs provided, and never in a pocket.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gelatinous cube|Walk the corridors pole-first, always: the pole comes back wet, and cheap. What the cube keeps, the cube has priced for you; cut the buckle out from the back face and let the front keep its patience.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:grimlock|Stand still and it reads you anyway: heartbeat, breath, the creak of your straps. Walk upon wet stone, speak plainly to cover your gear, and take the rush upon the off-side, where its ear judges worst.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:bugbear|Its reach is a pace longer than any honest measure of its arms, so chalk your distance and add one. The ambush is always at the second door, never the first; the Guild has paid for the counting.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:goblin dreamer|It fights half here and half at the standing stone, and the half here is slow. Break the chalk in its hand early, and Keep the Old Things Off, held ready, keeps its dreams from leaning on yours.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ettercap|Count its flock before you touch a single strand, for the shepherd answers the fence-wire faster than the sheep. Fire ruins the silk and the silk is the pay; cut dry, roll tight.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:merrow|The harpoon comes at the lantern-bearer first, every time, so the lantern rides a pole upon the gunwale. Cut the line before you contest the pull; the tarn wins every tug of war.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:water weird|It strikes from the basin it is bound to, and its binding names the basin, never the road beside it. Read the old orders aloud if you can find them: members report it stands down for the length of the reading.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:cloaker|Count the cloaks upon any peg-rail you pass, and count again on the way out. Its moan unmans the unready, so plug one ear with wax and keep the other for your party.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:chuul|It takes the armoured arm first, reckoning it the threat, so offer the shield-side and pay it in wood. The pincer opens at the joint to a pry-bar, and nothing else the Guild has shipped.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shrieker|It is the deep's doorbell, and killing it rings it. Shade the lantern to a slit, walk the bed's far edge, and if it cries anyway, stand and meet what comes rather than be met running.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:violet fungus|It stands in the harmless rows on purpose, and the harmless rows lean away from it: read the lean, and prune with the pole-hook at full extension. The frond keeps its virtue dried, and its menace too, so glove the taking.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:grey ooze|The wet patch of floor with no drip above it is the ooze. Steel feeds it; deal in stone and fire, and enter ruined kit against the Guild's insurance the same day.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:otyugh|Never fight it in its midden, for the ground is its oldest ally. Bait it two rooms upwind with the ration nobody wanted, and mind the third leg: the kick comes from it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:duergar overseer|Break the whistle and the gang stops working; some of the gang remembers who it was, and the overseer knows it, and grows careless in the hurry. Silence the whistle first and the rest is drill-book.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:roper|The narrow place with the convenient pillar is the roper, and the pillar's shadow is wrong for the light. Burn the strands at half-reach and keep a knife for the one you missed; the eye closes to fire and to nothing else.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:drider|It fights from the ceiling of its own grief and hurries for nothing. Foul the anchor-lines high on the wall before the first arrow, and it must come down to you, where the drill against spiders serves.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:black pudding|Report the flow's width to the member behind you before you do anything else about it. It pours downhill and through gratings, so hold the high ground and spend fire; a blade given to it is a donation.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gibbering mouther|Wax in both ears, chalk arrows before the ground goes soft, and one member told off to watch boots and nothing else. The babble borrows voices you know; the Guild's advice is written, for this floor, on paper.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shard-thrall|The body is a cart and the stone is the carter, so break the vein of grown stone at the shoulder and the cart is a body again, tired past fighting. Members are reminded the passenger was once somebody's file at the counter.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:invisible stalker|Flour, dust, or the fine grey spore of the vaults: throw it high and fight the shape the air makes. It leaves upon the instant its errand is done, so the surest defence is learning what was asked of it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:spirit naga|Its first working is always the one that holds you still, so break the eye-line at the first syllable. The fang is drawn with the grain and paid at the counter under bond, against the owner's return.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:mimic|Touch nothing in the halls with a bare hand until it has been touched with the pole. The tongue-tip is bought dried, and the drying is the seller's own affair.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:doppelganger|Agree a password with your party before entering the town, and use it upon everyone you know. The face is carried to the counter face-down, and Master Pell turns it over once, for the record.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:counterfeit burgher|The townsfolk below give the hour when asked, and the counterfeit gives it seven minutes early, upon the false bell. Ask, then strike upon the answer.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:clockwork bailiff|Let it read its warrant entire: the mainspring drives the reading or the truncheon, never both at once. The matter is settled from behind the shoulder while the law is being observed.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:cockatrice|The bite stones a limb by degrees, and the degrees can be outrun: bind above the bite, keep the limb below the heart, and climb. The tailfeather is drawn from the dead bird at the counter's price.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:griffon|It stoops from behind the lamps, so hood the lantern at the cage-rows and listen for the rasp of feather upon brass. A wing-feather in fair condition is bought for the fletchers by the piece.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:hell hound|Silent at the course and alight at the last length: when the straw smokes, throw yourself flat and cut upward. Draw the fang with tongs and carry it in sand, for the heat outlasts the beast by a day.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gorgon|The breath falls as a fog and settles grey: hold the breath you own, work upwind, and strike behind the foreleg where the plates part. The scales are bought by weight and want no polishing.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:copy-thrall|It must finish the line it is upon before it turns, so strike upon the turn. Spill the ink and it stops to blot. The inked cuff is bought at the counter as evidence of the hand.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ink elemental|It must keep to its channels or dry, so draw it out upon the dry flags and let the floor do half the work. The phial is filled at arm's length and stoppered wet.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shelf-warden|It strikes no body that is reshelving: carry a book, carry it visibly, and put it back wrong. While the warden rights your error, the matter is settled from behind.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:unbound working|It keeps its circuit to the minute, and the survey has the timetable. Stand aside for its passing and take it at the turn, where the old dismissal was cut to catch it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:glass double|Stand at the lamp with it: your eyes water and the double's stay dry. Strike the dry-eyed one, and let the true reflection find its own way back to the glass.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:spectre|Iron and courtesy both pass through it, and the old rites alone give it pause. Members without a working of the rites keep a wall at their back and their business brief.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:quicksilver hound|The blade parts it and buys one breath while it gathers: spend the breath on ground, never on a second stroke. The bead is taken up with a horn spoon and never with the fingers.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:medusa|Work by the mirrors, for the galleries are hung with them by an arrangement the collection respects. The hair is cut with shears, coiled with the grain, and boxed before it stills.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:waxling|Warm it and it slows: herd it toward your own lamp and away from the cold air, and take the wing whole once the wax runs soft at the edges.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:flesh golem|It follows its last order to the letter, so change what the letter requires: clear the table where you stand and it stands down while you pass. Stitching is cut, never unpicked, and bought by the hand's-length.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:half-made servitor|The unfinished side is the safe side: the smooth face keeps no eye and the short arm keeps no grip. Circle to its want of parts and work there.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:stone golem|Count the statuary at the door and count it again at the far arch, and the figure that has changed rank is the golem. The maul marks it where the sword rings off, so bring the maul.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:lens-warden|Hood the lantern and it comes down the stair for a better look, and it comes down within reach. The lens is taken from its collar in a gloved hand. A warm lens is paid double while it still shows the last thing it saw.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:loosed armature|It keeps the sweep it was cut for, so learn the arc and stand inside it where the arm carries no edge. Dent the pivot and the sweep runs short. The gear is drawn from the joint, and it is the same gear every time.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:cast-off apprentice|Watch the left hand: it closes a heartbeat before every working lands, the master's own habit, poured true. Move upon the closing, and the working spends itself where you stood.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:unsleeping warden|It defends the door and nothing beside: draw it one pace past the lintel and its orders march it back again. The fight is won in single paces, each taken while it returns to its post. The key is entered as Guild property upon recovery.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:vacant usher|It admits and turns back by an order only it keeps, so give it no order to read: enter by the servants' door it was never set to watch, and take it from behind while it waits upon precedence. The glove is drawn off cold and entered at the counter under seal.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shadow|It peels from the unlit wall, so keep the lamp between yourself and every dark span and cross no ground your own light has not touched. A Light That Won't Gutter, held ready, holds the wall lit and the shade flat against it. The strength it drains returns with a night's rest and not before.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:court-crier|Let it cry its order of business to the end: the bell drives the voice or the blow, never both at once, and the matter is settled while the season is being called. The handbell is bought cracked as it is found, for a bell mended rings for the next crier.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ghost|Iron passes through it and courtesy passes through it, so keep a wall at your back and your business brief, for it walks the shut doors as freely as the open. Borrowed Saint, spoken plainly, gives it pause for the length of the speaking. The ageing of its touch is entered against the Guild's insurance the same day.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:roll-binder|It binds what it finds loose among the stacks, so bind nothing of your own to a shelf and keep your hands from the open volumes. Foul its thread at the spool before it works, and it must set the binding to rights before it sets upon you. The thread is bought on the spool, dry.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:struck clerk|Its pale ink strikes as a stroke lands, and where the line falls the arm goes slack, so keep the bracers high and change your guard by turns. Break the eye-line at the first dip of the pen. The worn nib is drawn from the dead hand and paid by the dozen at the counter.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:roll-thrall|It swings the volume from the shoulder and is slow to recover it, so close upon the swing and stay inside the arc. The binding-leaf is bought by weight, less the weight of the words.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:night hag|She comes in the quiet after a rest to press the count, so bind your wounds and break camp before she can seat herself. Keep the Old Things Off, held ready, keeps her reckoning from leaning on your sleep. The tooth is drawn cold and entered under bond, against the debt she claimed.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:tomb-mason|It measures you for the eighth tomb before it lifts the maul, so give it no still moment to take the measure: keep moving, keep off the chalked line, and take it as it stoops to the plumb. Blunt work serves where the edge glances off dressed stone, so bring the mace. The chalk-line is bought whole.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:barrow-servant|The curse rides in the rot of the wrapping, so strike from upwind and do not close through the reek where the room gives you a way round. Fire ruins the linen and the linen is the pay, so cut dry and take the wrapping whole. Members schooled in Borrowed Saint report the stiffness passes the sooner.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:stone mourner|Leave an offering in its cupped hands, a numbered tag or a coin, and it kneels again to its post while you pass, for the fight is with the withholding and never the mourner. Where the offering is spent, the stone hands fall as a block falls, so take the first fall on a pillar. The hands are bought as dressed stone.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:mummy lord|It commands the lesser dead of the gallery, so fell the barrow-servants of the row before you close, or fight the lord and its servants both at once. Break the mask early, for the count it keeps is kept behind the mask. The grave-mask is drawn under three seals and entered to the survey's file upon the wardens.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:chain devil|The great chain and the devil are told apart only when the chain takes hold, so sound every span of the gallery chain with the pole before you pass it. Cut the barb, never the link, for a link parts slow and a barb looses at once. The chain-link is bought cold, the barbs drawn.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:debt-thrall|It fights to bind another in its place, so give it no purchase: keep the sprung shackle at pole's length and take the shoulder that drove the door. Break the term it drags and the thrall is a tired debtor again. Members are reminded the debtor was once somebody's line upon the Roll.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:tally-hound|It counts by scent and gives tongue at a name it cannot find, so foul your own scent upon the wet gutter-stone and cross its track on the down-side. Silence the collar's bell first and the pack behind it loses the count. The counting-collar is drawn whole and paid by the notch.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:bone devil|It herds before it kills and works the gallery on a warder's round, so break its round: take a cell at your back and force it to come straight. The sting turns the wound cold, so bind above the strike and keep the limb below the heart. Draw the hook with tongs and carry it in sand.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:works-tender|It will not leave the beam and the pumps to chase far, so stand off the machinery on the dry floor and make it come to you. Keep your feet clear of the wet channels, where it works surefooted and you do not. The oiled rag is bought by weight of the oil in it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:iron golem|It wakes only to a threat against the works, so threaten the works and take it as it turns: spike a gauge, and it must attend the needle first, the machinery outranking the caller in its orders. Iron cast so heavy turns the point, so meet it with the flat and the head. The ingot is bought as scrap of a good grade.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:water elemental|It must keep to the wet or fall to its own water, so draw it out onto the dry crown of the channels and let the floor do half the work. It takes the shield-side first, reckoning it the threat, so pay it in wood. The works-water is dipped at arm's length and stoppered wet.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:steam mephit|It bursts when it is broken, so break it at the length of a polearm and turn your face from it. Bleed a gauge-line and it goes to the leak before it goes to you. The scalded glass is bought whole and is worth nothing cracked.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:salamander|The coil sears through mail, so give it no coil: keep it at the sword's length and off the laid hearth, where its heat is least. Herd it from the mantel, for it fights hardest to keep the jar. Draw the ember-scale with tongs and bed it in sand until it will bear a hand.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:last clerk|It defends the record before its own unlife, so take the record from the desk and it must break its guard to keep it. The dried inkhorn is no weapon, but the pen it drives is, so close inside the reach of the arm. The inkhorn is entered as Guild property upon recovery.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:seal-keeper|It fights to keep the seventh seal from the striking, so make for the seventh: threaten the whole seal and it must stand before it rather than before you. The signet-die is heavy and slow, so take it on the turn. The die is drawn cold and entered under seal, the impression not to be taken.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:xorn|It rises through the floor to feed and sinks when it is full, so feed it: cast a coin wide and take it as it turns to the metal, for it holds the hoard dearer than the caller. Stone is no wall to it, so keep no wall at your back you have not sounded. The gem-set tooth is drawn from the fed beast and paid double.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sill-shade|Keep the lamp between yourself and every pooled dark at the sill, for it keeps off your own light. A Light That Won't Gutter, held ready, holds the threshold lit. It belongs to the door, so win past the sill and you have won the fight.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:brink-lurker|It works the very edge of the drop, so fight it a pace back from the sill, where its pull is its own and not the water's too. Sound your footing with the pole before every step. The lurker's grey claw is drawn cold and paid by the piece.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:spillwrack|Cut it, do not grapple it, for it winds the tighter the more you pull. Keep to the shallow of the fall where it drifts thin, and take it as it gathers. The wrack is bought dried, in a knot, and the drying is the seller's affair.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sluiceling|It rides the sheet of the fall, so leave the fall to it and fight upon the dry stone at the side. It strikes once with the water's whole weight and then goes on down, so take the first blow on a braced shield and it is spent. The wet husk is lifted whole and stoppered.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:earth elemental|It comes out of the wall you stand against, so set your back to open air and never to dressed stone, and strike it as it forms, before the rock has all drawn together. A Skin of Stone, held ready, turns the first blow. The core is drawn from the settled rubble and paid as raw stone.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:foundation-slough|It fills the gallery wall to wall, so take the high ground of a stair or a sill and keep it in front of you. Steel comes back pitted from it. The rind is dried and glazed before it will keep.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:foundation-wright|Keep moving along the wall it cuts and take it as it stoops to the line, for it is slow to leave a course half-dressed. Its reach is longer than its arms look, cut for work above its own head, so chalk your distance and add a pace. The inverted chisel is drawn from the drowned hand.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:drowned mason|It fights slow and defends the one course, so draw it off the wall it is cutting and its patience has nothing to keep. The mallet falls heavy and true but seldom, so spend the wait and strike between. The mallet is bought as drowned iron, less the water.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:blankling|It presses close to take the marks off you, so never let it set a hand: keep it at the pole's length and take it before it closes. Members report a working of the rites, spoken to name yourself aloud, keeps your own record while it strips at it. The smooth face is boxed and never handled bare.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:blank scrivener|Never let its cold hand settle, for a hand laid on is a record lifted: keep it at the pole's length and take it before it closes. Members carry a fair copy of their own papers sealed against the skin, that what it lifts may be entered again above. The blank margin is bought by the shred, dry.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:erasure|The wet-looking span of blank floor with no water above it is the erasure, so sound the floor with the pole and cross where it is dry. It unwrites the record but not the flesh, so a wound taken from it is a wound like any other; fight it as a slow tide and give ground. The smear is lifted on stone and never on steel.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:blank warden|It works upon what is written, so carry nothing written that you mean to keep, and set a blank leaf in its way instead. It stops to keep the blank leaf. The signature is bought uncut.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:fetterling|It is small and far stronger than its size, so do not close and grapple, but keep it at the pole and break the parted links it runs upon. It fastens as a fetter fastens, so cut the hold before it sets. The parted fetter is bought by the length, cold.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:anchorite|It bides at its anchor and reaches the length of its bond, so mark the anchors as you pass and keep beyond the reach of each. Cut the parted bond, not the thing, for the bond is where its strength runs. An anchorite's parted link is drawn cold and paid by the piece.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:behir|It runs the links faster than a horse and swallows whole, so give it no straight gallery to charge: fight it at a turn or an anchor, where its length works against it. Hold your breath through the first crack of its lightning-breath. The storm-scale is drawn cold and carried in oil, for the oil keeps the charge off the hand that bears it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:iron penitent|It fights to fasten its loosed chain upon another, so give it no purchase: keep the parted iron at the pole's length and take the shoulder that drags it. Break its grievance and the fight goes out of it, and it drags its iron a pace and stops. The parted iron is bought by weight, and the grievance is not the counter's affair.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:tally-shade|It draws across you like a mark tallied, in sevens, so count with it: six strokes, and the seventh is its whole strength, and the seventh is your opening. Break the eye-line at the first stroke. The tally-scratch is a rubbing, taken dry and paid to the survey.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:stir-scale|It surfaces only while the ground stirs, so if the bedrock lies still, stand still with it and let the breath fall even. Its plates are hot off the glow below, so take it on the shield and not the bare arm. The warm plate is drawn with tongs and set apart to cool.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:ground-drake|It comes up where the floor glows, so stand upon cold stone where the floor gives you the choice, and never stand still upon the warm. The molten plate is quenched before it will keep, and paid double.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sum-taker|It comes on slow and certain, so give ground and keep the middle distance and let the count spend itself upon your retreat. Break its eye from the tally at the first stroke. The counting-bead is drawn from the wire whole.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:coin-shade|It draws the warmth from a caller and sinks into the coin when it has fed, so give it no long hold: keep moving over the loose hoard and take it before it settles. Cold work slows it, and a torch held close drives it down into the coin. The warm coin it leaves is bought as coin, still ringing.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:purple worm|There is no quiet road past it, so mark the tremor of its swimming and fight where the coin lies shallow over stone, that it cannot dive beneath you. Take the sting well behind the barb and never squeeze it. The stinger is drawn standing clear, and the venom entered against the Guild's insurance.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:last debtor|It fights to keep the last count uncalled, so call it: press it toward the thing beneath, and it must stand between you and the count rather than come on. It is vast and slow, so keep the middle distance and spend the gaps. The final token is drawn from the fallen debt and entered under seal, the last such the Roll will take.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:hoard-golem|It gathers back into the hoard whatever it fells, so do not fall: keep your feet on the shifting coin and take it as it forms, before the gold has all run together. Fused coin turns a blade, so carry the mace, and mind your footing more than your edge. The fused coin is bought whole, by weight, and the hoard-fire is the survey's wonder, not the counter's.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sump crawler|Work it onto dry stone if the ground gives you leave, for away from the wet it is slow and half-blind. The shell parts cleanly at the seam behind the head. The slick of shell is bought whole, so lift it in one piece.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shale-lizard|The plated back turns most blows; take it underneath, where the shale gives way to soft hide, and let the tremor pass before you close. A scute prised from the living plate is bought at a good price.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:rimewing|Its wings will not bear it far from the cold. A torch held close drives it down, and the frost-dusted membrane tears at a touch once the beast is grounded. Take the wing entire, before the frost upon it melts.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gaol-crawler|It hunts only while the works' tremor holds, so if the deep stone stands still, stand still with it and let the beat fall even. Work it onto dry stone where you can, for away from the wet it is slow and half-blind. The rasped plate parts at the seam behind the head, and is bought whole, so lift it in one piece.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:coin-wyrm|It shows only in the dead calm, so if the deep air stirs, wait, for it will not surface. It basks in the glow and is slow to leave it, so come on it from the cold and take it before it dives. The gilded scale is struck from the same coin it swims, and is bought as coin and as trophy both.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gallows-crow|It strikes at the light and at the face, so set the lantern down at arm's length and fight beside it rather than behind it. The rasped tongue wants drying before it is packed, or it sours in a day.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:moor-hound|They come in threes, and the third holds off until the pair have closed, so break the third first. A clean cast of the peat-caked pad is worth more to the north survey than the pad itself.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:moor-skulker|It goes for the pack before the man, so let the pack fall and take it while it stoops. The peat-black pelt is bought cured, and a torn one is bought at nothing.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:giant bat|It strikes at whatever swings, so put the lantern down and leave it burning, and take the beast over the top of it. The wing-joint is bought whole and will not bear folding.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:milestone-shade|Answer with a road it knew and it lets you by; answer with a lie and it follows you to the next stone. Take the rubbing in wax, for ink runs on that stone in any weather.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:bog-lantern|Stand still and it comes to you, which is the only ground of your choosing you will get. It goes out at the first heavy blow, so lead with the heaviest you carry.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shutter-thrall|Call it by its name off the doorpost and it stops for a breath, and a breath is enough. The shutter-bar is oak and heavy and worth carrying only in a light pack.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:wererat|Silver, or the wound closes while you watch. It runs for the drains at half its strength, so put a body on the grate before you close.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:grave-verger|Take the bell-rope out of his hand first and the whole office falls apart, for he cannot call the rest without it. The frayed end is bought by the fathom.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:werebear|It works at close quarters and bears a man down, so keep the party spread and take it at the length of a spear. Silvered steel bites where plain steel slides. The clipped claw is bought by the pair.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:tallow-saint|Fire ruins the trophy and ends the fight, and the fight is the shorter matter, so use fire. Take the grey tallow from the base, where the wick was never lit.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:kennel-brute|The chain is still upon it and fouls at a corner, so fight it where the lane turns and not in the open. The kennel collar is bought as evidence, not as leather.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:twig blight|They stand among the true thorn and are known by the root-knot they walk on, so watch the ground rather than the branch. Dry twig burns, and a stand of them clears in one draw of flame.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:needle blight|They loose together, so never cross a ride in the open where two stands can see you. The sheaf of needles is bought by the apothecary and must be carried in a stiff case.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:harpy|The song comes before the stoop, so stop the ears of the party at the first note and keep moving under the boughs. The barred feather is bought by the piece and must be clean of blood.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:thorn-gibbet|It lifts what it takes clear of the ground, so keep a blade loose at the belt where a lifted hand can still reach it. The thorn grown through the iron is cut out with the iron and sold together.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:skin-changer|Silver, and mind the horn, for a horn from the crag brings it in rather than driving it off. The seamless pelt is proof of the thing and is bought as proof.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:green hag|Bargain if you must and hold her to the letter, for she holds you to yours. Fighting her in her own water is the worst ground in the Vale; draw her onto the ride.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:minotaur skeleton|It walks the rows and will not leave them, so break off across the ditch and it lets you go. Take it at the joint where the harness binds. The horn is bought whole and is worth nothing split.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:orchard-hand|It works the rows in their numbered order, so stand in a row it has already cut and it passes you by. The rusted hook is bought as abbey work and wants no cleaning.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:gnoll|They quarrel over the dig, so wait for the quarrel and take them apart in it. They will not follow past the wall. The scavenged jawbone is bought by the piece.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sprite|They shoot from the branches, so keep under the walls and out of the rows where the boughs meet overhead. The limed wing is bought flat between two boards.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:unshriven monk|It comes on with open hands and takes any answer for a refusal, so do not slow for the asking. The unsigned absolution is bought by the abbey's old table and is worth reading first.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:banshee|Stop the ears of the party before you enter the row, for the cry is the whole of the fight. A keening stoppered in a jar is worth a season's wages to the Guild library.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:jackalwere|Never take the guide it offers, and if you have taken it, leave the reeds by the way you came. Its jaw is bought as proof that the guide was refused.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:sea hag|Do not look at her when she rises; take her by the sound of the water instead. The drowned looking-glass is bought whole and is never looked into on the road.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:drowned bridesmaid|It reaches for a hand, so give it a blade and keep the hand. The sodden ribbon dries out white and is bought by the ell.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:reed-wraith|Its touch kills a limb to the shoulder, so fight it at length or not at all. A bundle of black reed keeps its cold for a day and is packed away from food.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:shambling mound|Fire is no use and lightning is worse, for both feed it; cut it apart and scatter the parts. Fen-thatch is bought by weight and comes up light once dried.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:nightmare|The hooves burn what they strike, so fight it off the turf, on stone or on standing water. A hoof-print burned into turf is lifted whole with the sod beneath it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:hunt-hound|It gives tongue only at the kill, so the first you hear of the pack is the last warning you get; watch the ground and not the air. The silvered collar is Corvane work and is bought at the castle's rate.

game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a sword of keen and uncommon sharpness|Recovered whole. The armourer's report reads: maker unknown, edge unaccountable, scabbard to be issued at the bearer's own risk.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a sealed potion of healing|The seal is the old Guild seal, three masters before the present one. The contents keep, and the Guild keeps the contents.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a great iron ring of keys|Fourteen keys upon one ring, none of which fits any lock the Guild owns. A standing order pays a bounty upon the finding of the fourteen doors.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a wizard's water-stained grimoire|Dried leaf by leaf at the Library's expense. Three pages remain shut fast to one another, and the copyists have stopped prying.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a copper scale of dragon-shed|Shed, by the assay, and never struck off. It sits warm in the case in every season, and the case is entered in the inventory as warm.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a gem-studded idol of a dead god|Catalogued as ornament, by order. The night-porter has asked that it face the wall, and the request is granted in the minutes.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:the dwarves' true survey map|The true survey, against which the Guild's own charts run three floors short. The corrections are entered in red, and there are many.

game/lowmoor.js|1|property:the False Guildmaster's pen|It writes in any hand it has once seen, and it is entered in the inventory as dry, to be kept so. The inventory is checked weekly.

game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a waxling's errand-list|Part-melted, and the last legible errand is a name and a floor. The name is entered elsewhere in this catalogue, and the floor is closed.

game/lowmoor.js|1|property:the Copyist-General's ribbon|The ribbon that ruled his page. The library measured it three times and got three lengths, and files the mean, under the measurer's written protest.

game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a season's docket from the court of the Lich|The order of business for one season of the lightless court, entered in a fair hand and complete to the last item. The final item is left blank, and the clerks decline to fill it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a creditor's tally-stick, notched past reading|A reckoning-stick of the eldest creditor, notched down its whole length and past all counting. The counting-house matched it against its own books, found the debt outstanding, and closed the ledger for the night.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a mourning-ring set with a grey stone|Gold, plain, and set with a grey stone that keeps no shine. The band is engraved within to a name the rolls do not carry, and the Guild keeps the ring against the day the name is entered.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a coin left upon a dead man's eye|One coin, of the old dead king's mint and warm at recovery like the rest of that hoard. The custom it comes from lies outside the counter's charter, and the coin is entered as a specimen and let lie.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a page torn from the roll below|A single leaf from the roll the court keeps of its own dead, torn at the binding. The names upon it are entered in the order they will die, and the ink of the last is not yet dry.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:the sexton's master key|The key opens every vault-door in the crypts, and one door besides that the survey has yet to find. It hangs with the other keys upon the ring, and the counter keeps it against the finding of that door.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a phial of tomb-lamp oil that will not gutter|Drawn from a tomb-lamp still burning after an age underground. Decanted, sealed and shelved; the sample burns yet in its phial, and the library keeps a wet cloth beside it as a rule.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a lodestone from the deep survey|A surveyor's lodestone, lost on some earlier descent and recovered far below where it was dropped. It points down, and holds to it however the case is turned.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a bell-clapper of cold iron|Struck from the tongue of a bell drowned before Mereward was named. It rings true when tapped, though no bell in the Guildhouse answers, and the counter keeps it wrapped in felt.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a stub of grave-lamp wick, still black|The burnt end of a wick from the court's funeral lamps, recovered cold. Set to a flame at the counter, it took the light at once and gave off no smoke, and was put out again by agreement.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:the night-porter's ward-list|The porter's own list of the treasures kept facing the wall, in the porter's own hand. It names one shelf the catalogue does not, and the porter has asked that the list be kept facing the wall with the rest.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a struck-through page of the Roll|A single leaf of the manifest of the sealed, a warden entered at its head in a firm hand and struck through at its foot in a fainter one. The Library reads the two hands against its own rolls, finds the warden a capstone the survey has on its books, and files the leaf under the count.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a warden's seal struck through in lead|One of the seven seals of the wardens, its impression whole and its field struck across in soft lead, in the fashion of the vault wall. The assay counter rules the lead a later hand and the seal itself older than the Guild, and keeps it against the reading of the impression.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a link of the great chain, drawn open|A single link of the restraint that ran the chained dark, the girth of a cart and drawn open at its ring rather than broken. The counter weighs it by the crane and enters the warden's signature its anchor carried beside the weight, and the survey has asked that the cut end be left as it was found.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a founder's mark rubbing, cut from below|A rubbing taken from a keystone of the drowned foundation, the founder's mark cut upside down to a reader coming from above and in no script the Guild's oldest books carry. The library files it against the Undergate arch's own marks, finds the hand the elder, and renews the filing whenever the arch is recut.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a silver-backed looking-glass, cracked across|Cracked corner to corner and silvered on the back in the old manner. The Guild's note is short and is repeated on the label: it shows the room correctly and it shows the holder correctly, and the two do not always agree upon how many people are in the room.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a phial of consecrated water, still sealed|Drawn and blessed at Saint Ordel before the abbey fell, and sealed with wax that carries the abbot's own thumb. The Guild keeps it sealed. There has been one application to open it, and it was refused in writing.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a mourning-brooch woven of dark hair|Woven of one person's hair and set behind glass, with a date upon the back and no name. Eleven of these have come out of the Vale and every one of them carries the same date.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a wolfsbane posy, dried and bound|Cut, dried and bound in the manner the village keeps, and hung by the door rather than worn. The Guild's herbal rules it worthless against any beast in the book, and the Guild has bought every one offered.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a coachman's brass lamp, unlit|The lamp is trimmed and filled, and the wick is white. The coach it belongs to stands in the stable-yard of the crag with its harness laid out and no horses in the Vale to put in it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a wedding-ring taken out of the fen|Plain silver, worn thin, and cut clean through as every ring out of that lake is cut. The inside carries a date, and the dates upon them run from three hundred years ago to the year before last.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a stake of white thorn, cut and cured|Cut from the Thornwake, seasoned two years and pointed by somebody who had done it before. The Guild lists it under implements rather than under arms, and lists no use beside it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a portrait-miniature of a lady long dead|A lady of the house in her wedding linen, painted from life by a good hand. The gallery upon the crag hangs the same face at full length, and in that canvas she is standing at the edge of the water.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a bell out of the abbey's fallen tower|Dug out of the rubble of Saint Ordel's tower, cracked at the lip and sound elsewhere. It rings when the cold moves it, which the survey notes is not a thing bells do.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:a hunting-horn mounted with the Count's crow|Mounted in silver with the crow of Corvane cut into the boss. Blown correctly it calls the pack off; blown incorrectly it calls the pack in, and the Guild's note upon the label says which is which.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:the baptismal register of Saint Ordel|Every soul of that valley entered in the abbey's own hand, the earliest of them older than the castle upon the crag. It names a child christened Aurel of the house that took the name Corvane after, and the library keeps the leaf under glass and has never printed it.
game/lowmoor.js|1|property:the reliquary of Saint Ordel|Silver-gilt and sound, seal whole, drawn up out of Ysmere on a drag-line after three hundred years in water that gives nothing back. The chapel of Castle Corvane was raised around this box, and the box was in the lake, and the chapel was shuttered from within.
